qman\+\_\+affine\+\_\+channel -\/ return the channel ID of an portal @cpu\+: the cpu whose affine portal is the subject of the query

If @cpu is -\/1, the affine portal for the current C\+PU will be used. It is a bug to call this function for any value of @cpu (other than -\/1) that is not a member of the mask returned from \mbox{\hyperlink{qman_8h_a378532a5903041f8a066f4282fba8620}{qman\+\_\+affine\+\_\+cpus()}}. \mbox{\Hypertarget{qman_8h_a378532a5903041f8a066f4282fba8620}\label{qman_8h_a378532a5903041f8a066f4282fba8620}}

\index{qman.h@{qman.h}!qman\_create\_cgr@{qman\_create\_cgr}}
\index{qman\_create\_cgr@{qman\_create\_cgr}!qman.h@{qman.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{qman\_create\_cgr()}{qman\_create\_cgr()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \mbox{\hyperlink{dcache_8h_ad65b480f8c8270356b45a9890f6499ae}{int}} qman\+\_\+create\+\_\+cgr (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structqman__cgr}{qman\+\_\+cgr}} $\ast$}]{cgr,  }\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{build_8c_a10e94b422ef0c20dcdec20d31a1f5049}{u32}}}]{flags,  }\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structqm__mcc__initcgr}{qm\+\_\+mcc\+\_\+initcgr}} $\ast$}]{opts }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

qman\+\_\+create\+\_\+cgr -\/ Register a congestion group object @cgr\+: the \textquotesingle{}cgr\textquotesingle{} object, with fields filled in @flags\+: Q\+M\+A\+N\+\_\+\+C\+G\+R\+\_\+\+F\+L\+A\+G\+\_\+$\ast$ values @opts\+: optional state of C\+GR settings

Registers this object to receiving congestion entry/exit callbacks on the portal affine to the cpu portal on which this A\+PI is executed. If opts is N\+U\+LL then only the callback (cgr-\/$>$cb) function is registered. If @flags \mbox{\hyperlink{protocolcontains-p}{contains}} Q\+M\+A\+N\+\_\+\+C\+G\+R\+\_\+\+F\+L\+A\+G\+\_\+\+U\+S\+E\+\_\+\+I\+N\+IT, then an init hw command (which will reset any unspecified parameters) will be used rather than a modify hw hardware (which only modifies the specified parameters). \mbox{\Hypertarget{qman_8h_a71a1b964257e447b4c4f391559d7b590}\label{qman_8h_a71a1b964257e447b4c4f391559d7b590}} 
\index{qman.h@{qman.h}!qman\_create\_fq@{qman\_create\_fq}}
\index{qman\_create\_fq@{qman\_create\_fq}!qman.h@{qman.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{qman\_create\_fq()}{qman\_create\_fq()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \mbox{\hyperlink{dcache_8h_ad65b480f8c8270356b45a9890f6499ae}{int}} qman\+\_\+create\+\_\+fq (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{build_8c_a10e94b422ef0c20dcdec20d31a1f5049}{u32}}}]{fqid,  }\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{build_8c_a10e94b422ef0c20dcdec20d31a1f5049}{u32}}}]{flags,  }\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structqman__fq}{qman\+\_\+fq}} $\ast$}]{fq }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

qman\+\_\+create\+\_\+fq -\/ Allocates a FQ @fqid\+: the index of the F\+QD to encapsulate, must be \char`\"{}\+Out of Service\char`\"{} @flags\+: bit-\/mask of Q\+M\+A\+N\+\_\+\+F\+Q\+\_\+\+F\+L\+A\+G\+\_\+$\ast$$\ast$$\ast$ options @fq\+: memory for storing the \textquotesingle{}fq\textquotesingle{}, with callbacks filled in

Creates a frame queue object for the given @fqid, unless the Q\+M\+A\+N\+\_\+\+F\+Q\+\_\+\+F\+L\+A\+G\+\_\+\+D\+Y\+N\+A\+M\+I\+C\+\_\+\+F\+Q\+ID flag is set in @flags, in which case a F\+Q\+ID is dynamically allocated (or the function fails if none are available). Once created, the caller should not touch the memory at \textquotesingle{}fq\textquotesingle{} except as extended to adjacent memory for user-\/defined fields (see the definition of \char`\"{}struct
qman\+\_\+fq\char`\"{} for more info). N\+O\+\_\+\+M\+O\+D\+I\+FY is only intended for enqueuing to pre-\/existing frame-\/queues that aren\textquotesingle{}t to be otherwise interfered with, it prevents all other modifications to the frame queue. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} T\+O\+\_\+\+D\+C\+P\+O\+R\+T\+AL flag causes the driver to honour any context\+\_\+b modifications requested in the qm\+\_\+init\+\_\+fq() A\+PI, as this indicates the frame queue will be consumed by a direct-\/connect portal (P\+ME, C\+A\+AM, or Fman). When frame queues are consumed by software portals, the context\+\_\+b field is controlled by the driver and can\textquotesingle{}t be modified by the caller. \mbox{\Hypertarget{qman_8h_aff6780729583faae9d99ea6b73aa3f68}\label{qman_8h_aff6780729583faae9d99ea6b73aa3f68}} 
\index{qman.h@{qman.h}!qman\_delete\_cgr@{qman\_delete\_cgr}}
\index{qman\_delete\_cgr@{qman\_delete\_cgr}!qman.h@{qman.h}}

qman\+\_\+delete\+\_\+cgr -\/ Deregisters a congestion group object @cgr\+: the \textquotesingle{}cgr\textquotesingle{} object to deregister

\char`\"{}\+Unplugs\char`\"{} this C\+GR object from the portal affine to the cpu on which this A\+PI is executed. This must be excuted on the same affine portal on which it was created. \mbox{\Hypertarget{qman_8h_a970d98188577f390b7763bb85ef3aed4}\label{qman_8h_a970d98188577f390b7763bb85ef3aed4}} 
\index{qman.h@{qman.h}!qman\_delete\_cgr\_safe@{qman\_delete\_cgr\_safe}}
\index{qman\_delete\_cgr\_safe@{qman\_delete\_cgr\_safe}!qman.h@{qman.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{qman\_delete\_cgr\_safe()}{qman\_delete\_cgr\_safe()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily void qman\+\_\+delete\+\_\+cgr\+\_\+safe (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structqman__cgr}{qman\+\_\+cgr}} $\ast$}]{cgr }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

qman\+\_\+delete\+\_\+cgr\+\_\+safe -\/ Deregisters a congestion group object from any C\+PU @cgr\+: the \textquotesingle{}cgr\textquotesingle{} object to deregister

This will select the proper C\+PU and run there \mbox{\hyperlink{qman_8h_aff6780729583faae9d99ea6b73aa3f68}{qman\+\_\+delete\+\_\+cgr()}}. \mbox{\Hypertarget{qman_8h_a07180995275c8fd9303b22b555749f52}\label{qman_8h_a07180995275c8fd9303b22b555749f52}}

qman\+\_\+destroy\+\_\+fq -\/ Deallocates a FQ @fq\+: the frame queue object to release

\mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} memory for this frame queue object (\textquotesingle{}fq\textquotesingle{} provided in \mbox{\hyperlink{qman_8h_a71a1b964257e447b4c4f391559d7b590}{qman\+\_\+create\+\_\+fq()}}) is not deallocated but the caller regains ownership, to do with as desired. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} FQ must be in the \textquotesingle{}out-\/of-\/service\textquotesingle{} or in the \textquotesingle{}parked\textquotesingle{} state. \mbox{\Hypertarget{qman_8h_abee292b9f5105f29079493fb49e1b8c5}\label{qman_8h_abee292b9f5105f29079493fb49e1b8c5}}

qman\+\_\+enqueue -\/ Enqueue a frame to a frame queue @fq\+: the frame queue object to enqueue to @fd\+: a descriptor of the frame to be enqueued

Fills an entry in the E\+Q\+CR of portal @qm to enqueue the frame described by @fd. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} descriptor details are copied from @fd to the E\+Q\+CR entry, the \textquotesingle{}pid\textquotesingle{} field is ignored. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} return value is non-\/zero on error, such as ring full. \mbox{\Hypertarget{qman_8h_a4db5f2222c432e07ebb03545a9d559a2}\label{qman_8h_a4db5f2222c432e07ebb03545a9d559a2}}

\index{qman\_init\_fq@{qman\_init\_fq}!qman.h@{qman.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{qman\_init\_fq()}{qman\_init\_fq()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \mbox{\hyperlink{dcache_8h_ad65b480f8c8270356b45a9890f6499ae}{int}} qman\+\_\+init\+\_\+fq (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structqman__fq}{qman\+\_\+fq}} $\ast$}]{fq,  }\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{build_8c_a10e94b422ef0c20dcdec20d31a1f5049}{u32}}}]{flags,  }\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structqm__mcc__initfq}{qm\+\_\+mcc\+\_\+initfq}} $\ast$}]{opts }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

qman\+\_\+init\+\_\+fq -\/ Initialises FQ fields, leaves the FQ \char`\"{}parked\char`\"{} or \char`\"{}scheduled\char`\"{} @fq\+: the frame queue object to modify, must be \textquotesingle{}parked\textquotesingle{} or new. @flags\+: bit-\/mask of Q\+M\+A\+N\+\_\+\+I\+N\+I\+T\+F\+Q\+\_\+\+F\+L\+A\+G\+\_\+$\ast$$\ast$$\ast$ options @opts\+: the F\+Q-\/modification settings, as defined in the low-\/level A\+PI

\mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} @opts parameter comes from the low-\/level portal A\+PI. Select Q\+M\+A\+N\+\_\+\+I\+N\+I\+T\+F\+Q\+\_\+\+F\+L\+A\+G\+\_\+\+S\+C\+H\+ED in @flags to cause the frame queue to be scheduled rather than parked. NB, @opts can be N\+U\+LL.

Note that some fields and options within @opts may be ignored or overwritten by the driver;
\begin{DoxyEnumerate}
\item the \textquotesingle{}count\textquotesingle{} and \textquotesingle{}fqid\textquotesingle{} fields are always ignored (this operation only affects one frame queue\+: @fq).
\item the Q\+M\+\_\+\+I\+N\+I\+T\+F\+Q\+\_\+\+W\+E\+\_\+\+C\+O\+N\+T\+E\+X\+TB option of the \textquotesingle{}we\+\_\+mask\textquotesingle{} field and the associated \textquotesingle{}fqd\textquotesingle{} structure\textquotesingle{}s \textquotesingle{}context\+\_\+b\textquotesingle{} field are sometimes overwritten;
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item if @fq was not created with Q\+M\+A\+N\+\_\+\+F\+Q\+\_\+\+F\+L\+A\+G\+\_\+\+T\+O\+\_\+\+D\+C\+P\+O\+R\+T\+AL, then context\+\_\+b is initialised to a value used by the driver for demux.
\item if context\+\_\+b is initialised for demux, so is context\+\_\+a in case stashing is requested (see item 4). (So caller control of context\+\_\+b is only possible for T\+O\+\_\+\+D\+C\+P\+O\+R\+T\+AL frame queue objects.)
\end{DoxyItemize}
\item if @flags \mbox{\hyperlink{protocolcontains-p}{contains}} Q\+M\+A\+N\+\_\+\+I\+N\+I\+T\+F\+Q\+\_\+\+F\+L\+A\+G\+\_\+\+L\+O\+C\+AL, the \textquotesingle{}fqd\textquotesingle{} structure\textquotesingle{}s \textquotesingle{}dest\+::channel\textquotesingle{} field will be overwritten to match the portal used to issue the command. If the W\+E\+\_\+\+D\+E\+S\+T\+WQ write-\/enable bit had already been set by the caller, the channel workqueue will be left as-\/is, otherwise the write-\/enable bit is set and the workqueue is set to a default of 4. If the \char`\"{}\+L\+O\+C\+A\+L\char`\"{} flag isn\textquotesingle{}t set, the destination channel/workqueue fields and the write-\/enable bit are left as-\/is.
\item if the driver overwrites context\+\_\+a/b for demux, then if Q\+M\+\_\+\+I\+N\+I\+T\+F\+Q\+\_\+\+W\+E\+\_\+\+C\+O\+N\+T\+E\+X\+TA is set, the driver will only overwrite context\+\_\+a.\+address fields and will leave the stashing fields provided by the user alone, otherwise it will zero out the context\+\_\+a.\+stashing fields. 
\end{DoxyEnumerate}\mbox{\Hypertarget{qman_8h_a35cccf79bc0850717e3b84cf1b545337}\label{qman_8h_a35cccf79bc0850717e3b84cf1b545337}}

qman\+\_\+oos\+\_\+fq -\/ Puts a FQ \char`\"{}out of service\char`\"{} @fq\+: the frame queue object to be put out-\/of-\/service, must be \textquotesingle{}retired\textquotesingle{}

{\footnotesize\ttfamily \mbox{\hyperlink{dcache_8h_ad65b480f8c8270356b45a9890f6499ae}{int}} qman\+\_\+release\+\_\+fqid (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{build_8c_a10e94b422ef0c20dcdec20d31a1f5049}{u32}}}]{fqid }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

qman\+\_\+release\+\_\+fqid -\/ Release the specified frame queue ID @fqid\+: the F\+Q\+ID to be released back to the resource pool

This function can also be used to seed the allocator with F\+Q\+ID ranges that it can subsequently allocate from. Returns 0 on success, or a negative error code. \mbox{\Hypertarget{qman_8h_a98c4e5c753189319a137b2e73678c00c}\label{qman_8h_a98c4e5c753189319a137b2e73678c00c}} 
\index{qman.h@{qman.h}!qman\_release\_pool@{qman\_release\_pool}}
\index{qman\_release\_pool@{qman\_release\_pool}!qman.h@{qman.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{qman\_release\_pool()}{qman\_release\_pool()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \mbox{\hyperlink{dcache_8h_ad65b480f8c8270356b45a9890f6499ae}{int}} qman\+\_\+release\+\_\+pool (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{build_8c_a10e94b422ef0c20dcdec20d31a1f5049}{u32}}}]{id }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

qman\+\_\+release\+\_\+pool -\/ Release the specified pool-\/channel ID @id\+: the pool-\/chan ID to be released back to the resource pool

This function can also be used to seed the allocator with pool-\/channel ID ranges that it can subsequently allocate from. Returns 0 on success, or a negative error code. \mbox{\Hypertarget{qman_8h_a95b12f1118fc934f040522225e772196}\label{qman_8h_a95b12f1118fc934f040522225e772196}} 
\index{qman.h@{qman.h}!qman\_retire\_fq@{qman\_retire\_fq}}
\index{qman\_retire\_fq@{qman\_retire\_fq}!qman.h@{qman.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{qman\_retire\_fq()}{qman\_retire\_fq()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \mbox{\hyperlink{dcache_8h_ad65b480f8c8270356b45a9890f6499ae}{int}} qman\+\_\+retire\+\_\+fq (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structqman__fq}{qman\+\_\+fq}} $\ast$}]{fq,  }\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{build_8c_a10e94b422ef0c20dcdec20d31a1f5049}{u32}} $\ast$}]{flags }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

qman\+\_\+retire\+\_\+fq -\/ Retires a FQ @fq\+: the frame queue object to retire @flags\+: FQ flags (Q\+M\+A\+N\+\_\+\+F\+Q\+\_\+\+S\+T\+A\+T\+E$\ast$) if retirement completes immediately

Retires the frame queue. This returns zero if it succeeds immediately, +1 if the retirement was started asynchronously, otherwise it returns negative for failure. When this function returns zero, @flags is set to indicate whether the retired FQ is empty and/or whether it has any O\+RL fragments (to show up as E\+R\+Ns). Otherwise the corresponding flags will be known when a subsequent F\+Q\+RN message shows up on the portal\textquotesingle{}s message ring.

NB, if the retirement is asynchronous (the FQ was in the Truly Scheduled or Active state), the completion will be via the message ring as a F\+Q\+RN -\/ but the corresponding callback may occur before this function returns!! Ie. the caller should be prepared to accept the callback as the function is called, not only once it has returned. \mbox{\Hypertarget{qman_8h_a18370dd3a8656be906cb7aa6862bda9d}\label{qman_8h_a18370dd3a8656be906cb7aa6862bda9d}} 
\index{qman.h@{qman.h}!qman\_schedule\_fq@{qman\_schedule\_fq}}
\index{qman\_schedule\_fq@{qman\_schedule\_fq}!qman.h@{qman.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{qman\_schedule\_fq()}{qman\_schedule\_fq()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \mbox{\hyperlink{dcache_8h_ad65b480f8c8270356b45a9890f6499ae}{int}} qman\+\_\+schedule\+\_\+fq (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structqman__fq}{qman\+\_\+fq}} $\ast$}]{fq }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

qman\+\_\+schedule\+\_\+fq -\/ Schedules a FQ @fq\+: the frame queue object to schedule, must be \textquotesingle{}parked\textquotesingle{}

Schedules the frame queue, which must be Parked, which takes it to Tentatively-\/\+Scheduled or Truly-\/\+Scheduled depending on its fill-\/level. \mbox{\Hypertarget{qman_8h_a3954d8e3db71c4e395b0920d705f1318}\label{qman_8h_a3954d8e3db71c4e395b0920d705f1318}} 
\index{qman.h@{qman.h}!qman\_start\_using\_portal@{qman\_start\_using\_portal}}
\index{qman\_start\_using\_portal@{qman\_start\_using\_portal}!qman.h@{qman.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{qman\_start\_using\_portal()}{qman\_start\_using\_portal()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \mbox{\hyperlink{dcache_8h_ad65b480f8c8270356b45a9890f6499ae}{int}} qman\+\_\+start\+\_\+using\+\_\+portal (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct qman\+\_\+portal $\ast$}]{p,  }\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structdevice}{device}} $\ast$}]{dev }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

qman\+\_\+start\+\_\+using\+\_\+portal -\/ register a device link for the portal user {\ttfamily }\+: the portal that will be in use @dev\+: the device that will use the portal

Makes sure that the devices that use the portal are unbound when the portal is unbound \mbox{\Hypertarget{qman_8h_a2668886e8962bfbaef57c0d1c1fbc03f}\label{qman_8h_a2668886e8962bfbaef57c0d1c1fbc03f}}
